	% -----
	\subsubsection{John 14} % *JOHN-14 
	% -----
		\label{JOHN-14}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{John 14:1} % *JOHN-14-1 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-14-1}

			LET not your heart be troubled: ye believe in God, believe also in me.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Μὴ ταρασσέσθω ὑμῶν ἡ καρδία· πιστεύετε εἰς τὸν θεόν, καὶ εἰς ἐμὲ πιστεύετε.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 14:2} % *JOHN-14-2 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-14-2}

			In my Father's house are many mansions:%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					See \hyperref[DC98-18]{DC 98:18}.
					} 
			if it were not so, I would have told you. I go to prepare a place for you.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐν τῇ οἰκίᾳ τοῦ πατρός μου μοναὶ πολλαί εἰσιν· εἰ δὲ μή, εἶπον ἂν ὑμῖν ὅτι πορεύομαι ἑτοιμάσαι τόπον ὑμῖν·}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 14:3} % *JOHN-14-3 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-14-3}

			And if I go and prepare a place for you, I will come again, and receive you unto myself; that where I am, there ye may be also.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἐὰν πορευθῶ καὶ ἑτοιμάσω τόπον ὑμῖν, πάλιν ἔρχομαι καὶ παραλήμψομαι ὑμᾶς πρὸς ἐμαυτόν, ἵνα ὅπου εἰμὶ ἐγὼ καὶ ὑμεῖς ἦτε.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]

		% ---

			And whither I go ye know, and the way ye know.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ὅπου ἐγὼ ὑπάγω οἴδατε τὴν ὁδόν.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 14:5} % *JOHN-14-5 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-14-5}

			Thomas saith unto him, Lord, we know not whither thou goest; and how can we know the way?

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{λέγει αὐτῷ Θωμᾶς· Κύριε, οὐκ οἴδαμεν ποῦ ὑπάγεις· πῶς δυνάμεθα τὴν ὁδὸν εἰδέναι;}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 14:6} % *JOHN-14-6 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-14-6}

			Jesus saith unto him, I am the way, the truth, and the life:%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					For points related to this idea, see \hyperref[JOHN-5-26]{John 5:26} and notes there.
					}%
			no man cometh unto the Father, but by me.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{λέγει αὐτῷ ὁ Ἰησοῦς· Ἐγώ εἰμι ἡ ὁδὸς καὶ ἡ ἀλήθεια καὶ ἡ ζωή· οὐδεὶς ἔρχεται πρὸς τὸν πατέρα εἰ μὴ δι᾽ ἐμοῦ.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 14:7} % *JOHN-14-7 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-14-7}

			If ye had known me, ye should have known my Father also: and from henceforth ye know him, and have seen him.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἰ ἐγνώκειτέ με, καὶ τὸν πατέρα μου ἂν ᾔδειτε· ἀπ᾽ ἄρτι γινώσκετε αὐτὸν καὶ ἑωράκατε αὐτόν.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 14:8} % *JOHN-14-8 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-14-8}

			Philip saith unto him, Lord, shew us the Father, and it sufficeth us.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Λέγει αὐτῷ Φίλιππος· Κύριε, δεῖξον ἡμῖν τὸν πατέρα, καὶ ἀρκεῖ ἡμῖν.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 14:9} % *JOHN-14-9 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-14-9}

			Jesus saith unto him, Have I been so long time with you, and yet hast thou not known me, Philip? he that hath seen me hath seen the Father; and how sayest thou then, Shew us the Father?

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{λέγει αὐτῷ ὁ Ἰησοῦς· Τοσούτῳ χρόνῳ μεθ᾽ ὑμῶν εἰμι καὶ οὐκ ἔγνωκάς με, Φίλιππε; ὁ ἑωρακὼς ἐμὲ ἑώρακεν τὸν πατέρα· πῶς σὺ λέγεις· Δεῖξον ἡμῖν τὸν πατέρα;}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 14:10} % *JOHN-14-10 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-14-10}

			Believest thou not that I am in the Father, and the Father in me? the words that I speak unto you I speak not of myself: but the Father that dwelleth in me, he doeth the works.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὐ πιστεύεις ὅτι ἐγὼ ἐν τῷ πατρὶ καὶ ὁ πατὴρ ἐν ἐμοί ἐστιν; τὰ ῥήματα ἃ ἐγὼ λέγω ὑμῖν ἀπ᾽ ἐμαυτοῦ οὐ λαλῶ, ὁ δὲ πατὴρ ἐν ἐμοὶ μένων ποιεῖ τὰ ἔργα αὐτοῦ.}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]

			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 14:11} % *JOHN-14-11 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-14-11}

			Believe me that I am in the Father, and the Father in me: or else believe me for the very works' sake.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{πιστεύετέ μοι ὅτι ἐγὼ ἐν τῷ πατρὶ καὶ ὁ πατὴρ ἐν ἐμοί· εἰ δὲ μή, διὰ τὰ ἔργα αὐτὰ πιστεύετε.}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 14:12} % *JOHN-14-12 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-14-12}

			Verily, verily, I say unto you, He that believeth on me, the works that I do shall he do also; and greater works than these shall he do; because I go unto my Father.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀμὴν ἀμὴν λέγω ὑμῖν, ὁ πιστεύων εἰς ἐμὲ τὰ ἔργα ἃ ἐγὼ ποιῶ κἀκεῖνος ποιήσει, καὶ μείζονα τούτων ποιήσει, ὅτι ἐγὼ πρὸς τὸν πατέρα πορεύομαι·}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 14:13} % *JOHN-14-13 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-14-13}

			And whatsoever ye shall ask in my name, that will I do, that the Father may be glorified in the Son.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ὅ τι ἂν αἰτήσητε ἐν τῷ ὀνόματί μου τοῦτο ποιήσω, ἵνα δοξασθῇ ὁ πατὴρ ἐν τῷ υἱῷ·}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 14:14} % *JOHN-14-14 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-14-14}

			If ye shall ask any thing in my name, I will do it.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐάν τι αἰτήσητέ με ἐν τῷ ὀνόματί μου ἐγὼ ποιήσω.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 14:15} % *JOHN-14-15 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-14-15}

			If ye love me, keep my commandments.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ἐὰν ἀγαπᾶτέ με, τὰς ἐντολὰς τὰς ἐμὰς τηρήσετε·}

		\paragraph{John 14:16} % *JOHN-14-16 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-14-16}

			And I will pray the Father, and he shall give you another Comforter, that he may abide with you for ever;

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{κἀγὼ ἐρωτήσω τὸν πατέρα καὶ ἄλλον παράκλητον δώσει ὑμῖν ἵνα ᾖ μεθ᾽ ὑμῶν εἰς τὸν αἰῶνα,}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 14:17} % *JOHN-14-17 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-14-17}

			Even the Spirit of truth; whom the world cannot receive, because it seeth him not, neither knoweth him: but ye know him; for he dwelleth with you, and shall be in you.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{τὸ πνεῦμα τῆς ἀληθείας, ὃ ὁ κόσμος οὐ δύναται λαβεῖν, ὅτι οὐ θεωρεῖ αὐτὸ οὐδὲ γινώσκει· ὑμεῖς γινώσκετε αὐτό, ὅτι παρ᾽ ὑμῖν μένει καὶ ἐν ὑμῖν ἔσται.}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]

	\subsubsection{John 15} % *JOHN-15 
	% -----
		\label{JOHN-15}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{John 15:1} % *JOHN-15-1 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-15-1}

			I AM the true vine, and my Father is the husbandman.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ἐγώ εἰμι ἡ ἄμπελος ἡ ἀληθινή, καὶ ὁ πατήρ μου ὁ γεωργός ἐστιν·}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 15:2} % *JOHN-15-2 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-15-2}

			Every branch in me that beareth not fruit he taketh away: and every branch that beareth fruit, he purgeth%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					The word is \gr{καθαίρω}, whose base meaning is ``\textbf{to cause someth. to become clean, \textit{make clean}},'' and there's an extension of that meaning that applies to plants, ``\textbf{to remove superfluous growth from a plant, \textit{clear, prune}} of a vine'' (BDAG 432b). BDAG says that Philo uses the word this way. LSJ's first meaning is ``\textit{cleanse},'' or ``\textit{purify, refine}'' (Plato uses it this way), and including in a religious sense. LSJ \#I.4 is ``\textit{prune} a tree, \textit{clear} it of superfluous wood,'' but the only reference is this verse in John. My concordance tells me this is the only occurrence of \gr{καθαίρω} in the NT, and so it seems as though Christ is just applying the meaning of cleansing/purifying/refining to the situation of plants and vineyards (see \gr{καθαροί} in the next verse). I also think there's a bit of pun/wordplay going on here, because Christ says he's going to \gr{αἴρει} the branch that isn't bearing fruit, and \gr{καθαίρει} the branch that is, so that it can bring forth more.
					}
			it, that it may bring forth more fruit.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{πᾶν κλῆμα ἐν ἐμοὶ μὴ φέρον καρπὸν αἴρει αὐτό, καὶ πᾶν τὸ καρπὸν φέρον καθαίρει αὐτὸ ἵνα καρπὸν πλείονα φέρῃ.}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]

			If ye abide in me, and my words abide in you, ye shall ask what ye will, and it shall be done unto you.

					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐὰν μείνητε ἐν ἐμοὶ καὶ τὰ ῥήματά μου ἐν ὑμῖν μείνῃ, ὃ ἐὰν θέλητε αἰτήσασθε καὶ γενήσεται ὑμῖν·}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 15:8} % *JOHN-15-8 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-15-8}

				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					The way the SLBG gives the Greek text here, the KJV looks like a mistranslation. Based on that Greek it should be, ``In this is my Father glorified: that you bear much fruit, and that you become my disciples.'' So both the bearing fruit and the becoming disciples are the ways in which the Father is glorified. There is a different textual tradition where the word is \gr{γενησεσθε}, and that would be the future tense and so would be an independent clause, as the KJV gives it (although MTCGNT 246 has a footnote that says that the future indicative can sometimes follow \gr{ἵνα}). MTCGNT gives the \gr{γένησθε} reading a ``D'' level of certainty, which means super uncertain, and says it was very difficult to decide. My red NT upgrades the rating to a ``C,'' thought that still means they had trouble, and the blue edition also has \gr{γένησθε}. 
					}%
			Herein is my Father glorified, that ye bear much fruit; so shall ye be my disciples.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐν τούτῳ ἐδοξάσθη ὁ πατήρ μου ἵνα καρπὸν πολὺν φέρητε καὶ γένησθε ἐμοὶ μαθηταί.}

					% \item[NTXXXX*]

				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

			These things I command you, that%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					The various uses of \gr{ἵνα} in this chapter are all very interesting. Sometimes it gives content to what has been said before, like verse 12, and other times, like in this verse, it could be more of a purpose clause, so the \gr{ἵνα} here isn't giving content to the \gr{ταῦτα}, but the \gr{ταῦτα} is the summary of what's been said and he's said it \textit{so that} we love each other.
					} 
			ye love one another.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}

					% \item[AB ]

					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]

					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]

				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

	% -----
	\subsubsection{John 16} % *JOHN-16 
	% -----
		\label{JOHN-16}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:1} % *JOHN-16-1 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-1}

			THESE things have I spoken unto you, that ye should not be offended.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ταῦτα λελάληκα ὑμῖν ἵνα μὴ σκανδαλισθῆτε.}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:2} % *JOHN-16-2 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-2}

			They shall put you out of the synagogues: yea, the time cometh, that whosoever killeth you will think that he doeth God service.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀποσυναγώγους ποιήσουσιν ὑμᾶς· ἀλλ᾽ ἔρχεται ὥρα ἵνα πᾶς ὁ ἀποκτείνας ὑμᾶς δόξῃ λατρείαν προσφέρειν τῷ θεῷ.}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:3} % *JOHN-16-3 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-3}

			And these things will they do unto you, because they have not known the Father, nor me.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ταῦτα ποιήσουσιν ὅτι οὐκ ἔγνωσαν τὸν πατέρα οὐδὲ ἐμέ.}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:4} % *JOHN-16-4 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-4}

			But these things have I told you, that when the time shall come, ye may remember that I told you of them. And these things I said not unto you at the beginning, because I was with you.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀλλὰ ταῦτα λελάληκα ὑμῖν ἵνα ὅταν ἔλθῃ ἡ ὥρα αὐτῶν μνημονεύητε αὐτῶν ὅτι ἐγὼ εἶπον ὑμῖν. Ταῦτα δὲ ὑμῖν ἐξ ἀρχῆς οὐκ εἶπον, ὅτι μεθ᾽ ὑμῶν ἤμην.}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:5} % *JOHN-16-5 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-5}

			But now I go my way to him that sent me; and none of you asketh me, Whither goest thou?

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{νῦν δὲ ὑπάγω πρὸς τὸν πέμψαντά με καὶ οὐδεὶς ἐξ ὑμῶν ἐρωτᾷ με· Ποῦ ὑπάγεις;}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:6} % *JOHN-16-6 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-6}

			But because I have said these things unto you, sorrow hath filled your heart.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀλλ᾽ ὅτι ταῦτα λελάληκα ὑμῖν ἡ λύπη πεπλήρωκεν ὑμῶν τὴν καρδίαν.}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:7} % *JOHN-16-7 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-7}

			Nevertheless I tell you the truth; It is expedient for you that I go away: for if I go not away, the Comforter will not come unto you; but if I depart, I will send him unto you.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀλλ᾽ ἐγὼ τὴν ἀλήθειαν λέγω ὑμῖν, συμφέρει ὑμῖν ἵνα ἐγὼ ἀπέλθω. ἐὰν γὰρ μὴ ἀπέλθω, ὁ παράκλητος οὐ μὴ ἔλθῃ πρὸς ὑμᾶς· ἐὰν δὲ πορευθῶ, πέμψω αὐτὸν πρὸς ὑμᾶς.}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:8} % *JOHN-16-8 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-8}

			And when he is come, he will reprove%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					See \hyperref[ELENCHO]{\grl{ἐλέγχω}} at \hyperref[JOHN-3-20]{John 3:20}.
					}
			the world of sin, and of righteousness, and of judgment:

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἐλθὼν ἐκεῖνος} \hyperref[ELENCHO]{\grl{ἐλέγξει}} \gr{τὸν κόσμον περὶ ἁμαρτίας καὶ περὶ δικαιοσύνης καὶ περὶ κρίσεως·}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:9} % *JOHN-16-9 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-9}

			Of sin, because they believe not on me;

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{περὶ ἁμαρτίας μέν, ὅτι οὐ πιστεύουσιν εἰς ἐμέ·}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:10} % *JOHN-16-10 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-10}

			Of righteousness, because I go to my Father, and ye see me no more;

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{περὶ δικαιοσύνης δέ, ὅτι πρὸς τὸν πατέρα ὑπάγω καὶ οὐκέτι θεωρεῖτέ με·}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:11} % *JOHN-16-11 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-11}

			Of judgment, because the prince of this world is judged.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{περὶ δὲ κρίσεως, ὅτι ὁ ἄρχων τοῦ κόσμου τούτου κέκριται.}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:12} % *JOHN-16-12 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-12}

			I have yet many things to say unto you, but ye cannot bear them now.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ἔτι πολλὰ ἔχω ὑμῖν λέγειν, ἀλλ᾽ οὐ δύνασθε βαστάζειν ἄρτι·}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:13} % *JOHN-16-13 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-13}

			Howbeit when he, the Spirit of truth, is come, he will guide you into all truth: for he shall not speak of himself; but whatsoever he shall hear, that shall he speak: and he will shew you things to come.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὅταν δὲ ἔλθῃ ἐκεῖνος, τὸ πνεῦμα τῆς ἀληθείας, ὁδηγήσει ὑμᾶς ἐν τῇ ἀληθείᾳ πάσῃ, οὐ γὰρ λαλήσει ἀφ᾽ ἑαυτοῦ, ἀλλ᾽ ὅσα ἀκούσει λαλήσει, καὶ τὰ ἐρχόμενα ἀναγγελεῖ ὑμῖν.}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:14} % *JOHN-16-14 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-14}

			He shall glorify me: for he shall receive of mine, and shall shew it unto you.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐκεῖνος ἐμὲ δοξάσει, ὅτι ἐκ τοῦ ἐμοῦ λήμψεται καὶ ἀναγγελεῖ ὑμῖν.}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:15} % *JOHN-16-15 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-15}

			All things that the Father hath are mine: therefore said I, that he shall take of mine, and shall shew it unto you.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{πάντα ὅσα ἔχει ὁ πατὴρ ἐμά ἐστιν· διὰ τοῦτο εἶπον ὅτι ἐκ τοῦ ἐμοῦ λαμβάνει καὶ ἀναγγελεῖ ὑμῖν.}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:16} % *JOHN-16-16 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-16}

			A little while, and ye shall not see me: and again, a little while, and ye shall see me,
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					This ``because...'' part isn't in the SBLG. See the next verse, and also verse 10, which has a very similar ``because'' part.
					}%
			because I go to the Father.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Μικρὸν καὶ οὐκέτι θεωρεῖτέ με, καὶ πάλιν μικρὸν καὶ ὄψεσθέ με.}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:17} % *JOHN-16-17 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-17}

			Then said some of his disciples among themselves, What is this that he saith unto us, A little while, and ye shall not see me: and again, a little while, and ye shall see me: and, Because I go to the Father?

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἶπαν οὖν ἐκ τῶν μαθητῶν αὐτοῦ πρὸς ἀλλήλους· Τί ἐστιν τοῦτο ὃ λέγει ἡμῖν· Μικρὸν καὶ οὐ θεωρεῖτέ με, καὶ πάλιν μικρὸν καὶ ὄψεσθέ με; καί· Ὅτι ὑπάγω πρὸς τὸν πατέρα;}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:18} % *JOHN-16-18 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-18}

			They said therefore, What is this that he saith, A little while? we cannot tell what he saith.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἔλεγον οὖν· Τί ἐστιν τοῦτο ὃ λέγει μικρόν; οὐκ οἴδαμεν τί λαλεῖ.}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:19} % *JOHN-16-19 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-19}

			Now Jesus knew that they were desirous to ask him, and said unto them, Do ye enquire among yourselves of that I said, A little while, and ye shall not see me: and again, a little while, and ye shall see me?

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἔγνω Ἰησοῦς ὅτι ἤθελον αὐτὸν ἐρωτᾶν, καὶ εἶπεν αὐτοῖς· Περὶ τούτου ζητεῖτε μετ᾽ ἀλλήλων ὅτι εἶπον· Μικρὸν καὶ οὐ θεωρεῖτέ με, καὶ πάλιν μικρὸν καὶ ὄψεσθέ με;}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:20} % *JOHN-16-20 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-20}

			Verily, verily, I say unto you, That ye shall weep and lament, but the world shall rejoice: and ye shall be sorrowful, but your sorrow shall be turned into joy.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀμὴν ἀμὴν λέγω ὑμῖν ὅτι κλαύσετε καὶ θρηνήσετε ὑμεῖς, ὁ δὲ κόσμος χαρήσεται· ὑμεῖς λυπηθήσεσθε, ἀλλ᾽ ἡ λύπη ὑμῶν εἰς χαρὰν γενήσεται.}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:21} % *JOHN-16-21 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-21}

			A woman when she is in travail hath sorrow, because her hour is come: but as soon as she is delivered of the child, she remembereth no more the anguish, for joy that a man is born into the world.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἡ γυνὴ ὅταν τίκτῃ λύπην ἔχει, ὅτι ἦλθεν ἡ ὥρα αὐτῆς· ὅταν δὲ γεννήσῃ τὸ παιδίον, οὐκέτι μνημονεύει τῆς θλίψεως διὰ τὴν χαρὰν ὅτι ἐγεννήθη ἄνθρωπος εἰς τὸν κόσμον.}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:22} % *JOHN-16-22 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-22}

			And ye now therefore have sorrow: but I will see you again, and your heart shall rejoice, and your joy no man taketh from you.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ὑμεῖς οὖν νῦν μὲν λύπην ἔχετε· πάλιν δὲ ὄψομαι ὑμᾶς, καὶ χαρήσεται ὑμῶν ἡ καρδία, καὶ τὴν χαρὰν ὑμῶν οὐδεὶς αἴρει ἀφ᾽ ὑμῶν.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 16:23} % *JOHN-16-23 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-23}

			And in that day ye shall ask me nothing. Verily, verily, I say unto you, Whatsoever ye shall ask the Father in my name, he will give it you.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἐν ἐκείνῃ τῇ ἡμέρᾳ ἐμὲ οὐκ ἐρωτήσετε οὐδέν· ἀμὴν ἀμὴν λέγω ὑμῖν, ἄν τι αἰτήσητε τὸν πατέρα δώσει ὑμῖν ἐν τῷ ὀνόματί μου.}

		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-24}

			Hitherto have ye asked nothing in my name: ask, and ye shall receive, that your joy may be full.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἕως ἄρτι οὐκ ᾐτήσατε οὐδὲν ἐν τῷ ὀνόματί μου· αἰτεῖτε καὶ λήμψεσθε, ἵνα ἡ χαρὰ ὑμῶν ᾖ πεπληρωμένη.}

		\paragraph{John 16:25} % *JOHN-16-25 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-25}

			These things have I spoken unto you in proverbs: but the time cometh, when I shall no more speak unto you in proverbs, but I shall shew you plainly of the Father.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ταῦτα ἐν παροιμίαις λελάληκα ὑμῖν· ἔρχεται ὥρα ὅτε οὐκέτι ἐν παροιμίαις λαλήσω ὑμῖν ἀλλὰ παρρησίᾳ περὶ τοῦ πατρὸς ἀπαγγελῶ ὑμῖν.}

		% ---

			At that day ye shall ask in my name: and I say not unto you, that I will pray the Father for you:

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐν ἐκείνῃ τῇ ἡμέρᾳ ἐν τῷ ὀνόματί μου αἰτήσεσθε, καὶ οὐ λέγω ὑμῖν ὅτι ἐγὼ ἐρωτήσω τὸν πατέρα περὶ ὑμῶν·}

		% ---

			For the Father himself loveth you, because ye have loved me, and have believed that I came out from God.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{αὐτὸς γὰρ ὁ πατὴρ φιλεῖ ὑμᾶς, ὅτι ὑμεῖς ἐμὲ πεφιλήκατε καὶ πεπιστεύκατε ὅτι ἐγὼ παρὰ τοῦ θεοῦ ἐξῆλθον.}

		% ---

			I came forth from the Father, and am come into the world: again, I leave the world, and go to the Father.

					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐξῆλθον ἐκ τοῦ πατρὸς καὶ ἐλήλυθα εἰς τὸν κόσμον· πάλιν ἀφίημι τὸν κόσμον καὶ πορεύομαι πρὸς τὸν πατέρα.}

		% ---

			His disciples said unto him, Lo, now speakest thou plainly, and speakest no proverb.

		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-30}

			Now are we sure that thou knowest all things, and needest not that any man should ask thee: by this we believe that thou camest forth from God.

				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{νῦν οἴδαμεν ὅτι οἶδας πάντα καὶ οὐ χρείαν ἔχεις ἵνα τίς σε ἐρωτᾷ· ἐν τούτῳ πιστεύομεν ὅτι ἀπὸ θεοῦ ἐξῆλθες.}

		\paragraph{John 16:32} % *JOHN-16-32 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-32}

			Behold, the hour cometh, yea, is now come, that ye shall be scattered, every man to his own, and shall leave me alone: and yet I am not alone, because the Father is with me.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἰδοὺ ἔρχεται ὥρα καὶ ἐλήλυθεν ἵνα σκορπισθῆτε ἕκαστος εἰς τὰ ἴδια κἀμὲ μόνον ἀφῆτε· καὶ οὐκ εἰμὶ μόνος, ὅτι ὁ πατὴρ μετ᾽ ἐμοῦ ἐστιν.}

		% ---
			\label{JOHN-16-33}

			These things I have spoken unto you, that in me ye might have peace. In the world ye shall have tribulation: but be of good cheer;%

					See \hyperref[DC101-16]{DC 101:16}; 
					}
			I have overcome the world.

	\subsubsection{John 17} % *JOHN-17 
	% -----
		\label{JOHN-17}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{John 17:1} % *JOHN-17-1 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-17-1}

			THESE words spake Jesus, and lifted up his eyes to heaven, and said, Father, the hour is come; glorify thy Son, that thy Son also may glorify thee:

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ταῦτα ἐλάλησεν Ἰησοῦς, καὶ ἐπάρας τοὺς ὀφθαλμοὺς αὐτοῦ εἰς τὸν οὐρανὸν εἶπεν· Πάτερ, ἐλήλυθεν ἡ ὥρα· δόξασόν σου τὸν υἱόν, ἵνα ὁ υἱὸς δοξάσῃ σέ,}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 17:2} % *JOHN-17-2 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-17-2}

			As thou hast given him power over all flesh, that he should give eternal life to as many as thou hast given him.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καθὼς ἔδωκας αὐτῷ} \hyperref[EXOUSIA]{\grl{ἐξουσίαν}} \gr{πάσης σαρκός, ἵνα πᾶν ὃ δέδωκας αὐτῷ δώσῃ αὐτοῖς ζωὴν αἰώνιον.}
					% \item[SNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

		% ---

		% ---
			\label{JOHN-17-3}

				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					Compare \hyperref[DC132-24]{DC 132:24}.
					}%
			And this is life eternal, that they might know thee the only true God, and Jesus Christ, whom thou hast sent.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{αὕτη δέ ἐστιν ἡ αἰώνιος ζωὴ ἵνα γινώσκωσι σὲ τὸν μόνον ἀληθινὸν θεὸν καὶ ὃν ἀπέστειλας Ἰησοῦν Χριστόν.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 17:4} % *JOHN-17-4 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-17-4}

			I have glorified thee on the earth: I have finished the work which thou gavest me to do.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐγώ σε ἐδόξασα ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς, τὸ ἔργον} \hyperref[TELEIOO]{\grl{τελειώσας}} \gr{ὃ δέδωκάς μοι ἵνα ποιήσω·}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 17:5} % *JOHN-17-5 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-17-5}

			And now, O Father, glorify thou me with thine own self with the glory which I had with thee before the world was.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ νῦν δόξασόν με σύ, πάτερ, παρὰ σεαυτῷ τῇ δόξῃ ᾗ εἶχον πρὸ τοῦ τὸν κόσμον εἶναι παρὰ σοί.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 17:6} % *JOHN-17-6 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-17-6}

			I have manifested thy name unto the men which thou gavest me out of the world: thine they were, and thou gavest them me; and they have kept thy word.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ἐφανέρωσά σου τὸ ὄνομα τοῖς ἀνθρώποις οὓς ἔδωκάς μοι ἐκ τοῦ κόσμου. σοὶ ἦσαν κἀμοὶ αὐτοὺς ἔδωκας, καὶ τὸν λόγον σου τετήρηκαν.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]

		\paragraph{John 17:7} % *JOHN-17-7 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-17-7}

			Now they have known that all things whatsoever thou hast given me are of thee.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{νῦν ἔγνωκαν ὅτι πάντα ὅσα δέδωκάς μοι παρὰ σοῦ εἰσιν·}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 17:8} % *JOHN-17-8 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-17-8}

			For I have given unto them the words which thou gavest me; and they have received them, and have known surely that I came out from thee, and they have believed that thou didst send me.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὅτι τὰ ῥήματα ἃ ἔδωκάς μοι δέδωκα αὐτοῖς, καὶ αὐτοὶ ἔλαβον καὶ ἔγνωσαν ἀληθῶς ὅτι παρὰ σοῦ ἐξῆλθον, καὶ ἐπίστευσαν ὅτι σύ με ἀπέστειλας.}

		\paragraph{John 17:9} % *JOHN-17-9 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-17-9}

			I pray for them: I pray not for the world, but for them which thou hast given me; for they are thine.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐγὼ περὶ αὐτῶν ἐρωτῶ· οὐ περὶ τοῦ κόσμου ἐρωτῶ ἀλλὰ περὶ ὧν δέδωκάς μοι, ὅτι σοί εἰσιν,}

		% ---
			\label{JOHN-17-10}

			And all mine are thine, and thine are mine; and I am glorified in them.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ τὰ ἐμὰ πάντα σά ἐστιν καὶ τὰ σὰ ἐμά, καὶ δεδόξασμαι ἐν αὐτοῖς.}

			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 17:11} % *JOHN-17-11 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-17-11}

			And now I am no more in the world, but these are in the world, and I come to thee. Holy Father, keep through thine own name those whom thou hast given me, that they may be one, as we are.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ οὐκέτι εἰμὶ ἐν τῷ κόσμῳ, καὶ αὐτοὶ ἐν τῷ κόσμῳ εἰσίν, κἀγὼ πρὸς σὲ ἔρχομαι. πάτερ ἅγιε, τήρησον αὐτοὺς ἐν τῷ ὀνόματί σου ᾧ δέδωκάς μοι, ἵνα ὦσιν ἓν καθὼς ἡμεῖς.}

		\paragraph{John 17:12} % *JOHN-17-12 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-17-12}

			While I was with them in the world, I kept them in thy name: those that thou gavest me I have kept, and none of them is lost, but the son of perdition; that the scripture might be fulfilled.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὅτε ἤμην μετ᾽ αὐτῶν ἐγὼ ἐτήρουν αὐτοὺς ἐν τῷ ὀνόματί σου ᾧ δέδωκάς μοι, καὶ ἐφύλαξα, καὶ οὐδεὶς ἐξ αὐτῶν ἀπώλετο εἰ μὴ ὁ υἱὸς τῆς ἀπωλείας, ἵνα ἡ γραφὴ πληρωθῇ.}

		% ---

				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{νῦν δὲ πρὸς σὲ ἔρχομαι, καὶ ταῦτα λαλῶ ἐν τῷ κόσμῳ ἵνα ἔχωσιν τὴν χαρὰν τὴν ἐμὴν πεπληρωμένην ἐν ἑαυτοῖς.}

		% ---
			\label{JOHN-17-14}

			I have given them thy word; and the world hath hated them, because they are not of the world, even as I am not of the world.

				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐγὼ δέδωκα αὐτοῖς τὸν λόγον σου, καὶ ὁ κόσμος ἐμίσησεν αὐτούς, ὅτι οὐκ εἰσὶν ἐκ τοῦ κόσμου καθὼς ἐγὼ οὐκ εἰμὶ ἐκ τοῦ κόσμου.}

		% ---

			I in them, and thou in me, that they may be made perfect in one; and that the world may know that thou hast sent me, and hast loved them, as thou hast loved me.

			\begin{framed}

				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐγὼ ἐν αὐτοῖς καὶ σὺ ἐν ἐμοί, ἵνα ὦσιν τετελειωμένοι εἰς ἕν, ἵνα γινώσκῃ ὁ κόσμος ὅτι σύ με ἀπέστειλας καὶ ἠγάπησας αὐτοὺς καθὼς ἐμὲ ἠγάπησας.}

		% ---
			\label{JOHN-17-24}

			Father, I will that they also, whom thou hast given me, be with me where I am; that they may behold my glory, which thou hast given me: for thou lovedst me before the foundation of the world.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{πάτερ, ὃ δέδωκάς μοι, θέλω ἵνα ὅπου εἰμὶ ἐγὼ κἀκεῖνοι ὦσιν μετ᾽ ἐμοῦ, ἵνα θεωρῶσιν τὴν δόξαν τὴν ἐμὴν ἣν δέδωκάς μοι, ὅτι ἠγάπησάς με πρὸ καταβολῆς κόσμου.}

	\subsubsection{John 18} % *JOHN-18 
	% -----
		\label{JOHN-18}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{John 18:1} % *JOHN-18-1 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-18-1}

			WHEN Jesus had spoken these words, he went forth with his disciples over the brook Cedron, where was a garden, into the which he entered, and his disciples.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ταῦτα εἰπὼν Ἰησοῦς ἐξῆλθεν σὺν τοῖς μαθηταῖς αὐτοῦ πέραν τοῦ χειμάρρου τοῦ Κεδρὼν ὅπου ἦν κῆπος, εἰς ὃν εἰσῆλθεν αὐτὸς καὶ οἱ μαθηταὶ αὐτοῦ.}

				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ᾔδει δὲ καὶ Ἰούδας ὁ παραδιδοὺς αὐτὸν τὸν τόπον, ὅτι πολλάκις συνήχθη Ἰησοῦς ἐκεῖ μετὰ τῶν μαθητῶν αὐτοῦ.}

				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὁ οὖν Ἰούδας λαβὼν τὴν σπεῖραν καὶ ἐκ τῶν ἀρχιερέων καὶ ἐκ τῶν Φαρισαίων ὑπηρέτας ἔρχεται ἐκεῖ μετὰ φανῶν καὶ λαμπάδων καὶ ὅπλων.}

			They answered him, Jesus of Nazareth. Jesus saith unto them, I am he. And Judas also, which betrayed him, stood with them.

				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀπεκρίθησαν αὐτῷ· Ἰησοῦν τὸν Ναζωραῖον. λέγει αὐτοῖς· Ἐγώ εἰμι. εἱστήκει δὲ καὶ Ἰούδας ὁ παραδιδοὺς αὐτὸν μετ᾽ αὐτῶν.}

		\paragraph{John 18:6} % *JOHN-18-6 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-18-6}

			As soon then as he had said unto them, I am he, they went backward, and fell to the ground.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὡς οὖν εἶπεν αὐτοῖς· Ἐγώ εἰμι, ἀπῆλθον εἰς τὰ ὀπίσω καὶ ἔπεσαν χαμαί.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]

					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀπεκρίθη ὁ Πιλᾶτος· Μήτι ἐγὼ Ἰουδαῖός εἰμι; τὸ ἔθνος τὸ σὸν καὶ οἱ ἀρχιερεῖς παρέδωκάν σε ἐμοί· τί ἐποίησας;}

		\paragraph{John 18:36} % *JOHN-18-36 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-18-36}

			Jesus answered, My kingdom is not of this world: if my kingdom were of this world, then would my servants fight, that I should not be delivered to the Jews: but now is my kingdom not from hence.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀπεκρίθη Ἰησοῦς· Ἡ βασιλεία ἡ ἐμὴ οὐκ ἔστιν ἐκ τοῦ κόσμου τούτου· εἰ ἐκ τοῦ κόσμου τούτου ἦν ἡ βασιλεία ἡ ἐμή, οἱ ὑπηρέται οἱ ἐμοὶ ἠγωνίζοντο ἄν, ἵνα μὴ παραδοθῶ τοῖς Ἰουδαίοις· νῦν δὲ ἡ βασιλεία ἡ ἐμὴ οὐκ ἔστιν ἐντεῦθεν.}

		% ---
			\label{JOHN-18-37}

			Pilate therefore said unto him, Art thou a king then? Jesus answered, Thou sayest that I am a king. To this end was I born, and for this cause came I into the world, that I should bear witness unto the truth. Every one that is of the truth heareth my voice.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἶπεν οὖν αὐτῷ ὁ Πιλᾶτος· Οὐκοῦν βασιλεὺς εἶ σύ; ἀπεκρίθη ὁ Ἰησοῦς· Σὺ λέγεις ὅτι βασιλεύς εἰμι. ἐγὼ εἰς τοῦτο γεγέννημαι καὶ εἰς τοῦτο ἐλήλυθα εἰς τὸν κόσμον ἵνα μαρτυρήσω τῇ ἀληθείᾳ· πᾶς ὁ ὢν ἐκ τῆς ἀληθείας ἀκούει μου τῆς φωνῆς.}

			Pilate saith unto him, What is truth? And when he had said this, he went out again unto the Jews, and saith unto them, I find in him no fault at all.

					\item[SBLG] \gr{λέγει αὐτῷ ὁ Πιλᾶτος· Τί ἐστιν ἀλήθεια; Καὶ τοῦτο εἰπὼν πάλιν ἐξῆλθεν πρὸς τοὺς Ἰουδαίους, καὶ λέγει αὐτοῖς· Ἐγὼ οὐδεμίαν εὑρίσκω ἐν αὐτῷ αἰτίαν·}

		% ---

		% ---
			\label{JOHN-19-6}

			When the chief priests therefore and officers saw him, they cried out, saying, Crucify him, crucify him. Pilate saith unto them, Take ye him, and crucify him: for I find no fault in him.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὅτε οὖν εἶδον αὐτὸν οἱ ἀρχιερεῖς καὶ οἱ ὑπηρέται ἐκραύγασαν λέγοντες· Σταύρωσον σταύρωσον. λέγει αὐτοῖς ὁ Πιλᾶτος· Λάβετε αὐτὸν ὑμεῖς καὶ σταυρώσατε, ἐγὼ γὰρ οὐχ εὑρίσκω ἐν αὐτῷ αἰτίαν.}

		% ---
			\label{JOHN-19-9}

			And went again into the judgment hall, and saith unto Jesus, Whence art thou? But Jesus gave him no answer.

			\begin{framed}

			When Jesus therefore had received the vinegar, he said, It is finished: and he bowed his head, and gave up the ghost.

					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὅτε οὖν ἔλαβεν τὸ ὄξος ὁ Ἰησοῦς εἶπεν· Τετέλεσται, καὶ κλίνας τὴν κεφαλὴν παρέδωκεν τὸ πνεῦμα.}

		\paragraph{John 19:31} % *JOHN-19-31 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-19-31}

			The Jews therefore, because it was the preparation, that the bodies should not remain upon the cross on the sabbath day, (for that sabbath day was an high day,) besought Pilate that their legs might be broken, and that they might be taken away.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Οἱ οὖν Ἰουδαῖοι, ἐπεὶ παρασκευὴ ἦν, ἵνα μὴ μείνῃ ἐπὶ τοῦ σταυροῦ τὰ σώματα ἐν τῷ σαββάτῳ, ἦν γὰρ μεγάλη ἡ ἡμέρα ἐκείνου τοῦ σαββάτου, ἠρώτησαν τὸν Πιλᾶτον ἵνα κατεαγῶσιν αὐτῶν τὰ σκέλη καὶ ἀρθῶσιν.}

				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἦλθον οὖν οἱ στρατιῶται, καὶ τοῦ μὲν πρώτου κατέαξαν τὰ σκέλη καὶ τοῦ ἄλλου τοῦ συσταυρωθέντος αὐτῷ·}

		% ---
			\label{JOHN-19-38}

			And after this Joseph of Arimathæa, being a disciple of Jesus, but secretly for fear of the Jews, besought Pilate that he might take away the body of Jesus: and Pilate gave him leave. He came therefore, and took the body of Jesus.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Μετὰ δὲ ταῦτα ἠρώτησεν τὸν Πιλᾶτον Ἰωσὴφ ἀπὸ Ἁριμαθαίας, ὢν μαθητὴς τοῦ Ἰησοῦ κεκρυμμένος δὲ διὰ τὸν φόβον τῶν Ἰουδαίων, ἵνα ἄρῃ τὸ σῶμα τοῦ Ἰησοῦ· καὶ ἐπέτρεψεν ὁ Πιλᾶτος. ἦλθεν οὖν καὶ ἦρεν τὸ σῶμα αὐτοῦ.}

			THE first day of the week cometh Mary Magdalene early, when it was yet dark, unto the sepulchre, and seeth the stone taken away from the sepulchre.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Τῇ δὲ μιᾷ τῶν σαββάτων Μαρία ἡ Μαγδαληνὴ ἔρχεται πρωῒ σκοτίας ἔτι οὔσης εἰς τὸ μνημεῖον, καὶ βλέπει τὸν λίθον ἠρμένον ἐκ τοῦ μνημείου.}

			Then she runneth, and cometh to Simon Peter, and to the other disciple, whom Jesus loved, and saith unto them, They have taken away the Lord out of the sepulchre, and we know not where they have laid him.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{τρέχει οὖν καὶ ἔρχεται πρὸς Σίμωνα Πέτρον καὶ πρὸς τὸν ἄλλον μαθητὴν ὃν ἐφίλει ὁ Ἰησοῦς, καὶ λέγει αὐτοῖς· Ἦραν τὸν κύριον ἐκ τοῦ μνημείου, καὶ οὐκ οἴδαμεν ποῦ ἔθηκαν αὐτόν.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 20:23} % *JOHN-20-23 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-20-23}

			Whose soever sins ye remit, they are remitted unto them; and whose soever sins ye retain,%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					This is a somewhat uncommon use of \gr{κρατέω}, which BDAG at meaning \#7 (500a) gives as ``\textbf{to cause a condition to continue, \textit{hold in place}}.'' It then glosses this verse, the only verse listed for that meaning, as ``pronounce the sins unforgiven.'' Meaning \#IV.3 in LSJ is ``\textit{hold up, support, maintain, hold fast, keep, retain},'' which is at least in the ballpark, though maybe not exactly the same. Does this mean that the disciples had total control over what was a sin for someone during a repentance? I don't think so, but you'd have to do some thinking about this verse.
					}
			they are retained.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἄν τινων ἀφῆτε τὰς ἁμαρτίας ἀφέωνται αὐτοῖς· ἄν τινων κρατῆτε κεκράτηνται.}

				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%

					}%

				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἔλεγον οὖν αὐτῷ οἱ ἄλλοι μαθηταί· Ἑωράκαμεν τὸν κύριον. ὁ δὲ εἶπεν αὐτοῖς· Ἐὰν μὴ ἴδω ἐν ταῖς χερσὶν αὐτοῦ τὸν τύπον τῶν ἥλων καὶ βάλω τὸν δάκτυλόν μου εἰς τὸν τύπον τῶν ἥλων καὶ βάλω μου τὴν χεῖρα εἰς τὴν πλευρὰν αὐτοῦ, οὐ μὴ πιστεύσω.}

		\paragraph{John 20:30} % *JOHN-20-30 
		% ---

		\paragraph{John 21:15} % *JOHN-21-15

			\label{JOHN-21-15}

			So when they had dined, Jesus saith to Simon Peter, Simon, son of Jonas,
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					For discussion of the three questions, see verse 17.
					}%
			lovest thou me more than
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					``These'' being the people around him? The fish maybe? NIVAC NT 01 4607: ``We should keep in mind that these verses are closely linked with the fishing miracle of 21:1--14. Thus, when Jesus asks Peter, `Do you love me more than these,' we are forced to examine the context to unravel the meaning of `these.' Is Jesus asking, `Do you love me more than you love these boats and fish?' Or, `do you love me more than you love these men with whom you are working?' These two options are relevant since Peter has rejoined his fishing career and now with his friends around him, perhaps Jesus is calling him to make a choice. Does he love his career or is he willing to be Christ's disciple with a thoroughgoing call to ministry?'' Another part of NIVAC suggests Christ might be asking whether Peter loves him (Christ) \textit{more than the other men there love him}, suggesting that the questions are asking Peter to examine his commitment after the threefold denial earlier.
					}%
			these? He saith unto him, Yea, Lord; thou knowest that I love thee. He saith unto him, Feed my lambs.

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 21:17} % *JOHN-21-17 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-21-17}

			He saith unto him the third time, Simon, son of Jonas, lovest thou me? Peter was grieved because he said unto him the third time,
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					In Greek the question is different all three times, and note how Peter's response uses a different word from Christ's in the first two:
						\begin{compactdesc}
						\item[Verse 15] \gr{ἀγαπᾷς με πλέον τούτων;}
							\begin{compactdesc}
								\item[Peter] \gr{Ναί, κύριε, σὺ οἶδας ὅτι φιλῶ σε.}
							\end{compactdesc}
						\item[Verse 16] \gr{ἀγαπᾷς με;}
							\begin{compactdesc}
								\item[Peter] \gr{Ναί, κύριε, σὺ οἶδας ὅτι φιλῶ σε.}
							\end{compactdesc}
						\item[Verse 17] \gr{φιλεῖς με;}
							\begin{compactdesc}
								\item[Peter] \gr{Κύριε, πάντα σὺ οἶδας, σὺ γινώσκεις ὅτι φιλῶ σε.}
							\end{compactdesc}
						\end{compactdesc}
					So Christ asks a different question each time, and Peter always uses \gr{φιλέω} in his response, and never responds with \gr{ἀγαπάω}. A few quotations:
						\begin{compactdesc}
							\item[Verse 15] See note in verse 15, where I talk about how the three questions from Christ may echo Peter's three earlier denials.
							\item[ZIBBC NT 02 337] ``Jesus' threefold repetition of his question may reflect the Near Eastern custom of reiterating a matter three times before witnesses in order to convey a solemn obligation, especially with regard to contracts conferring rights or legal dispositions. By analogy, Jesus' threefold question lends solemnity to the occasion of his commissioning Peter to his special shepherding task.''
							\item[NIVAC NT 01 4609--4610] ``This has led many exegetes to see the variation between \textit{agapao} and \textit{phileo} as insignificant and to understand these two words as synonyms. In the LXX, for instance, Jacob's love for Joseph is expressed with both verbs (\hyperref[GENESIS-37-3]{Gen. 37:3--4}). In \hyperref[PROVERBS-8-17]{Proverbs 8:17} both words are used to represent one Hebrew verb. This is the view of major lexicons as well as grammarians. N. Turner refers to John's regular use of `needless' synonyms in order to bring variety to his narratives. John has two words each for love, send, heal, ask, speak, do, feed, sheep, and know, and in most cases these variations seem to merely avoid monotony. Leon Morris echoes this sentiment: `There is no difference on the grounds of Johannine usage, for seeing a difference in meaning between these two verbs.' The focus of Peter's conversation with Jesus has to do with his commission to tend the flock of Christ, not the quality of his love for him. Peter is thus upset (21:17) not because Jesus has changed the verb for love, but because Jesus has asked him the same question for the third time. The same sort of comment can be said for the charges given to Peter following each affirmation of love. Once again there is variation, but these word shifts bear no theological importance...Attempts to find deeper meaning in the connections among these words should be viewed with utmost caution.''
						\end{compactdesc}
					That NIVAC quotation is preceded by a fuller discussion---it talks about the possible difference between \gr{φιλέω} and \gr{ἀγαπάω}, and the corresponding possible differences in the verbs and nouns Christ uses to talk about the sheep. Ultimately NIVAC, citing various commentaries, wants to chalk the differences up to Johannine variation, which you see in a lot of other places, where John uses different words to change up the word choice while intending no difference in meaning. The passage in NIVAC finishes with, ``Attempts to find deeper meaning in the connections among these words should be viewed with utmost caution.'' That seems right to me. The text can only bear so much exegesis, and its tolerance depends on a lot of factors, including the author's style.
					}%

		% ---
		\paragraph{John 21:18} % *JOHN-21-18 
		% ---
			\label{JOHN-21-18}

			Verily, verily, I say unto thee, When thou wast young, thou girdedst thyself, and walkedst whither thou wouldest: but when thou shalt be old,
			\footnote{%
				% \label{}%
				Note the possible reference to Peter's death on a cross of his own. NIVAC NT 01 4610--4611: ``While some interpreters think that Jesus is simply referring to Peter's dependence, a strong case can be made for typical Johannine double meaning. In another day, someone will `stretch out your hands,' Jesus says to Peter. This language points clearly to crucifixion; the Greek word used here was used by many early Christian writers to represent death on the cross. If this is true---and I am convinced it is---then we may also have one more allusion to crucifixion in 21:18. In old age, Peter will not be `dressed' (NIV), but `bound' (Gk. \textit{zonnyo}), as many victims were `fastened' with ropes to the cross. Those who lead him will not be charitable, but will force him to go where he does not want to. We should no doubt read these verses together with \hyperref[JOHN-13-36]{13:36--38}, where Peter makes his bold promise to `lay down his life' for Jesus. Indeed, Jesus promises, while Peter cannot go where Jesus is going now, Peter will `follow later.' Jesus is anticipating his own hour of glorification, and both in 13:37 and in 21:18 he is prophesying Peter's fate. Peter will lay down his life for Jesus (13:38); he will be crucified too; he will likewise glorify God in the same manner as his Lord---but this must await a time in the future. `Follow me' in 21:19 now takes on a poignant and provocative new meaning. Peter will follow Jesus to the cross.'' For the Greek for ``stretch forth,'' see \gr{ἐκτείνω} at BDAG 274a. That entry does note at the end of meaning \#1 that the word can be used ``of one who is crucified,'' and it cites a few authorities. This connection is fairly loose but it reminds me of the endowment ceremony in two ways: with the second token of the Melchizedek priesthood, and with what Adam does at the beginning of the \hyperref[TEMPLE-ENDOWMENT-ENDOW-PRE-1990-TELESTIAL]{telestial} part of the endowment.
				}%
